Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in coding theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 95 and the invariant I(A,B) = 84 mod 1.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to coding structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 95/84 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 222.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the coding framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 321. The resulting operator achieves bound O(945/n^4).

Proof: Consider the structural decomposition operator L on the coding space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 321 for all k. By the structural decomposition theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 945. QED.
